Appendix B
Proofs

B.1
Proof of Lemma
4.2

Proof.

Consider task
k
. Recall that
\mathcal{D}_{k}
contains
n_{k}
data points.
According to Theorem 3.2 in
[
10
]
, we have with probability at least
1-\delta/m
, for all
\mathbf{w}
,

\left|\mathcal{L}_{k}(\mathbf{w},\mathfrak{D}_{k})-\mathcal{L}_{k}(\mathbf{w},\mathcal{D}_{k})\right|\leq 2R_{n_{k}}(\mathcal{H})+\sqrt{\frac{\ln(2m/\delta)}{2n_{k}}}\leq\frac{2C}{n_{k}^{\alpha}}+\sqrt{\frac{\ln(2m/\delta)}{2n_{k}}},

where the second inequality is by the assumption on
R_{n}(\mathcal{H})
.
Combining the inequalities above with
\mathbf{w}=\mathbf{w}_{\text{new}}
and
\mathbf{w}=\mathbf{w}_{\text{old}}
, we have with probability at least
1-\delta/m

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{k}(\mathbf{w}_{\text{new}},\mathfrak{D}_{k})-\mathcal{L}_{k}(\mathbf{w}_{\text{old}},\mathfrak{D}_{k})\leq\mathcal{L}_{k}(\mathbf{w}_{\text{new}},\mathcal{D}_{k})-\mathcal{L}_{k}(\mathbf{w}_{\text{old}},\mathcal{D}_{k})+\frac{4C}{n_{k}^{\alpha}}+2\sqrt{\frac{\ln(2m/\delta)}{2n_{k}}}.

Applying the union bound with the events above for
k=1,\dots,m
leads to the conclusion of this lemma.
∎

B.2
Proof of Lemma
5.3

Proof.

Recall that
\mathbf{w}
has two component
\mathbf{u}
and
W
.
The gradient of
h_{k}(\mathbf{w})
with respect to
W
and
\mathbf{u}
are denoted by

\nabla_{W}h_{k}(\mathbf{w})
and
\nabla_{\mathbf{u}}h_{k}(\mathbf{w})
, respectively. Hence,

\nabla h_{k}(\mathbf{w})=\left(\nabla_{\mathbf{u}}h_{k}(\mathbf{w}),\nabla_{W}h_{k}(\mathbf{w})\right)

for
k=1,\dots,m
. Similarly, after adding the task-dependent heads,
\hat{\mathbf{w}}
has four component
\mathbf{u}
,
W
,
\mathbf{U}
and
\mathbf{V}
. The gradients
\nabla_{\mathbf{u}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})
,
\nabla_{W}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})

\nabla_{\mathbf{U}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})
and
\nabla_{\mathbf{V}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})
are defined correspondingly, and

\nabla\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})=\left(\nabla_{\mathbf{u}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}),\nabla_{W}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}),\nabla_{\mathbf{U}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}),\nabla_{\mathbf{V}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})\right).

Recall that

\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})=h_{k}(W+U_{k}V_{k}^{\top},\mathbf{u})\text{ for }k=1,\dots,m.

Therefore,

\nabla_{\mathbf{u}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})=\nabla_{\mathbf{u}}h_{k}(W+U_{k}V_{k}^{\top},\mathbf{u}),\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\nabla_{W}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})=\nabla_{W}h_{k}(W+U_{k}V_{k}^{\top},\mathbf{u})

and

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{U}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathbf{0},\dots,\mathbf{0},\underbrace{\nabla_{W}h_{k}(\mathbf{W}+U_{k}V_{k}^{\top},\mathbf{u})V_{k}}_{\text{The }k\text{th block}},\mathbf{0},\dots,\mathbf{0}\bigg)^{\top}

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{V}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathbf{0},\dots,\mathbf{0},\underbrace{\nabla_{W}h_{k}(\mathbf{W}+U_{k}V_{k}^{\top},\mathbf{u})^{\top}U_{k}}_{\text{The }k\text{th block}},\mathbf{0},\dots,\mathbf{0}\bigg)^{\top},

where the sparsity patterns of
\nabla_{\mathbf{U}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})
and
\nabla_{\mathbf{V}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})
are because
\hat{h}_{k}
does not depend on
U_{j}
and
V_{j}
with
j\neq k
.

Suppose
U_{k}V_{k}^{\top}=\mathbf{0}
for all
k
. It holds that
h_{k}(\mathbf{w})=\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})
and

\nabla h_{k}(\mathbf{w})=\left(\nabla_{\mathbf{u}}h_{k}(\mathbf{w}),\nabla_{W}h_{k}(\mathbf{w})\right)=\left(\nabla_{\mathbf{u}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}),\nabla_{W}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})\right).

Consider any
\boldsymbol{\alpha}=(\alpha_{1},\dots,\alpha_{m})\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
. We have

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\left(\left[\nabla\hat{h}_{1}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}),\dots,\nabla\hat{h}_{m}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})\right]^{\top}\left[\nabla\hat{h}_{1}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}),\dots,\nabla\hat{h}_{m}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min_{\boldsymbol{\alpha},\text{s.t.}\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}\|=1}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{m}\alpha_{k}\nabla\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min_{\boldsymbol{\alpha},\text{s.t.}\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}\|=1}\left(\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{m}\alpha_{k}\nabla_{\mathbf{u}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{m}\alpha_{k}\nabla_{W}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{m}\alpha_{k}\nabla_{\mathbf{U}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{m}\alpha_{k}\nabla_{\mathbf{V}}\hat{h}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{w}})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min_{\boldsymbol{\alpha},\text{s.t.}\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}\|=1}\left(\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{m}\alpha_{k}\nabla h_{k}(\mathbf{w})\right\|^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}\alpha_{k}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{W}h_{k}(\mathbf{w})V_{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}\alpha_{k}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{W}h_{k}(\mathbf{w})^{\top}U_{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\left(\left[\nabla h_{1}(\mathbf{w}),\dots,\nabla h_{m}(\mathbf{w})\right]^{\top}\left[\nabla h_{1}(\mathbf{w}),\dots,\nabla h_{m}(\mathbf{w})\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\min_{k}\left\|\nabla_{W}h_{k}(\mathbf{w})V_{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\min_{k}\left\|\nabla_{W}h_{k}(\mathbf{w})^{\top}U_{k}\right\|_{F}^{2},

where the first two equalities are by definitions and the third equality is because
U_{k}V_{k}^{\top}=\mathbf{0}
for all
k
.

∎

B.3
Proof of Theorem
1

In this section, we present the proof of the Theorem
1
. Recall that the problem is formulated as

\min_{{\mathbf{w}}}\ F({\mathbf{w}},\mathcal{D}):=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum^{n_{0}}_{i=1}\left(f(g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}))+f(g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}))\right)\quad\text{s.t.}\quad\frac{1}{m}h_{k}({\mathbf{w}};\mathcal{D}_{k})\leq 0,\ k=1,\cdots,m.

(13)

with
f(\cdot)=\tau\log(\cdot)
. With the quadratic penalty method, the problem is converted to

\min_{{\mathbf{w}}}\ \Phi({\mathbf{w}}):=F({\mathbf{w}},\mathcal{D})+\underbrace{\frac{1}{m}\sum_{k=1}^{m}\frac{\beta}{2}([h_{k}({\mathbf{w}};\mathcal{D}_{k})]_{+})^{2}}_{H({\mathbf{w}})}.

(14)

By Assumptions
1
, we can get
f
is
L_{f}
-Lipschitz continuous and
L_{\nabla f}
-smooth with
L_{f}=\frac{\tau}{c_{g}}
and
L_{\nabla f}=\frac{\tau}{c_{g}^{2}}
. By noticing that
\ell_{ce}
is a cross entropy loss, we find that
|h_{k}(\cdot)|
can be bounded by a constant
C_{h}
with
C_{h}=2
. Then, we can get
\Phi({\mathbf{w}})
is
L_{\beta}
-smooth with
L_{\beta}:=L_{F}+\beta L_{H}
where
L_{F}:=2(L_{\nabla g}L_{f}+L_{\nabla f}L_{g}^{2})
and
L_{H}:=L_{\nabla h}C_{h}+L_{h}^{2}
. We also define
\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}:=\sigma_{\nabla g}+L_{g}
and
\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}:=\sigma_{\nabla h}+L_{h}
. To facilitate our discussion, we let

\begin{split}v_{1}^{t}&=(1-\theta)v_{1}^{t-1}+\theta G_{1}^{t},\\
v_{2}^{t}&=(1-\theta)v_{2}^{t-1}+\theta G_{2}^{t},\\
v^{t}&=v_{1}^{t}+v_{2}^{t}.\end{split}

To prove our main theorem, we need following lemmas.

Lemma 5
.

If
\theta\leq\frac{1}{3}
, the gradient variance
\Delta_{1}^{t}:=\|v_{1}^{t}-\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t},\mathcal{D})\|^{2}
can be bounded as

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{1}^{t+1}]\leq&(1-\theta)\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{1}^{t}]+\frac{2L_{F}^{2}}{\theta}\mathbb{E}[\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}]+5\theta L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\Xi^{t+1}_{1}+\Xi^{t+1}_{2}]\\
&+3L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left(\left\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left\|u_{2i}^{t+1}-u_{2i}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]+\frac{2\theta^{2}L_{f}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla g}^{2}+L_{g}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}|,|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}},\end{split}

(15)

with
\Xi^{t+1}_{1}:=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t+1}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{0}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}
and
\Xi^{t+1}_{2}:=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\|\textbf{u}_{2}^{t+1}-\textbf{g}_{2}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{0}}\|u_{2i}^{t+1}-g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}
.

Proof.

\begin{split}\Delta_{1}^{t+1}&=\|v_{1}^{t+1}-\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}=\|(1-\theta)v_{1}^{t}+\theta G_{1}^{t}-\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\\
&=\left\|\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {

We can also get

\begin{split}&(1+\theta)\|\hbox to10.64pt{\vbox to10.64pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.31987pt\lower-5.31987pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.08 0 C 7.08 3.91 3.91 7.08 0 7.08 C -3.91 7.08 -7.08 3.91 -7.08 0 C -7.08 -3.91 -3.91 -7.08 0 -7.08 C 3.91 -7.08 7.08 -3.91 7.08 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.31248pt}{-2.9pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}=(1+\theta)(1-\theta)^{2}\|v_{1}^{t}-\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}\leq(1-\theta)\|v_{1}^{t}-\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}\\
&2\left(1+\frac{1}{\theta}\right)\left\|\hbox to10.64pt{\vbox to10.64pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.31987pt\lower-5.31987pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.08 0 C 7.08 3.91 3.91 7.08 0 7.08 C -3.91 7.08 -7.08 3.91 -7.08 0 C -7.08 -3.91 -3.91 -7.08 0 -7.08 C 3.91 -7.08 7.08 -3.91 7.08 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.31248pt}{-2.9pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\right\|^{2}=2\left(1+\frac{1}{\theta}\right)(1-\theta)^{2}\|\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\leq\frac{2L_{F}^{2}}{\theta}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}\end{split}

\begin{split}&\frac{2+3\theta}{\theta}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\|\hbox to10.64pt{\vbox to10.64pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.31987pt\lower-5.31987pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.08 0 C 7.08 3.91 3.91 7.08 0 7.08 C -3.91 7.08 -7.08 3.91 -7.08 0 C -7.08 -3.91 -3.91 -7.08 0 -7.08 C 3.91 -7.08 7.08 -3.91 7.08 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.31248pt}{-2.9pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}\right]\\
&=\frac{2+3\theta}{\theta}\frac{\theta^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}|}\mathbb{E}_{t}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left(\left\|\nabla\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\left\|\nabla f(u_{1i}^{t})-\nabla f(g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla\hat{g}_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\left\|\nabla f(u_{2i}^{t})-\nabla f(g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\right)\end{split}

We first bound the first term

\begin{split}&\frac{(2+3\theta)\theta}{|\mathcal{B}|}\mathbb{E}_{t}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left\|\nabla\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\left\|\nabla f(u_{1i}^{t})-\nabla f(g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{(2+3\theta)\theta L_{f}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}|}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{k+1}}\left\|\nabla\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\left\|u_{1i}^{t}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\right]\\
&=(2+3\theta)\theta L_{f}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\left\|\nabla\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{k+1})\right\|^{2}|i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}\right]\left\|u_{1i}^{t}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{k+1})\right\|^{2}\right]\\
&\leq(2+3\theta)\theta L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left\|u_{1i}^{t}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\right]\\
&\leq(2+3\theta)\theta L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}\left((1+\delta)\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{0}}\left\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\right]+(1+1/\delta)\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{0}}\left\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]\right)\\
&=(2+3\theta)\theta L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}\left((1+\delta)\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{0}}\left\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-g_{i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\right]+(1+1/\delta)\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]\right)\end{split}

If
\theta\leq\frac{1}{3}
and
\delta=\frac{3\theta}{2}
, we have
(2+3\theta)\theta(1+\delta)\leq 5\theta
and
(2+3\theta)\theta(1+1/\delta)\leq 3
. And similarly, we can get the bound for the second term. Then, by combining them, we can get

\frac{2+3\theta}{\theta}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}\right]\leq 5\theta L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\Xi_{1}^{t+1}+\Xi_{2}^{t+1}]+3L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left(\left\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left\|u_{2i}^{t+1}-u_{2i}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)\right].

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\|\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}\right]\\
=&\theta^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\nabla\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{k+1})\nabla f(g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{k+1}))-\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{0}}\nabla g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\nabla f(g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}))\right\|^{2}\right]\\
&+\theta^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\nabla\hat{g}_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{k+1})\nabla f(g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}))-\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{0}}\nabla g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\nabla f(g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}))\right\|^{2}\right]\\
=&\theta^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\nabla\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\nabla f(g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}))-\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\nabla g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\nabla f(g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}))\right\|^{2}\right]\\
&+\theta^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\nabla g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\nabla f(g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}))-\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{0}}\nabla g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\nabla f(g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}))\right\|^{2}\right]\\
&+\theta^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\nabla\hat{g}_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\nabla f(g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}))-\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\nabla g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\nabla f(g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}))\right\|^{2}\right]\\
&+\theta^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\nabla g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\nabla f(g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}))-\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{0}}\nabla g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\nabla f(g_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}))\right\|^{2}\right]\\
\leq&\frac{2\theta^{2}L_{f}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla g}^{2}+L_{g}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}|,|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}.\end{split}

Therefore, we can get

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{1}^{t+1}]\leq&(1-\theta)\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{1}^{t}]+\frac{2L_{F}^{2}}{\theta}\mathbb{E}[\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}]+5\theta L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\Xi^{t+1}_{1}+\Xi^{t+1}_{2}]\\
&+3L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left(\left\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left\|u_{2i}^{t+1}-u_{2i}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]+\frac{2\theta^{2}L_{f}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla g}^{2}+L_{g}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}|,|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}.\end{split}

∎

Lemma 6
.

If
\gamma_{1}\leq 1/5
, function value variance
\Xi^{t}_{1}:=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}
can be bounded as

\mathbb{E}[\Xi^{t+1}_{1}]\leq\left(1-\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{4n_{0}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\Xi^{t}_{1}\right]+\frac{5n_{0}L_{g}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}]}{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}+\frac{2\gamma_{1}^{2}\sigma_{g}^{2}|\mathcal{B}|}{n_{0}|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|}-\frac{1}{4n_{0}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\|^{2}\right].

(16)

Lemma 7
.

If
\gamma_{1}\leq 1/5
, function value variance
\Xi^{t}_{2}:=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\|\textbf{u}_{2}^{t}-\textbf{g}_{2}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}
can be bounded as

\mathbb{E}[\Xi^{t+1}_{2}]\leq\left(1-\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{4n_{0}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\Xi^{t}_{2}\right]+\frac{5n_{0}L_{g}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}]}{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}+\frac{2\gamma_{1}^{2}\sigma_{g}^{2}|\mathcal{B}|}{n_{0}|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|}-\frac{1}{4n_{0}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{2i}^{t+1}-u_{2i}^{t}\|^{2}\right].

(17)

Since the proof of Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.3
are almost the same, we only presents the proof of Lemma
B.3
as follows.

Proof.

Define
\phi_{1}^{t}(\textbf{u}_{1})=\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}_{1}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{k})\|^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\sum^{n_{0}}_{i=1}\|u_{1i}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{k})\|^{2}
, which is
1
-strongly convex.

\begin{split}\phi^{t+1}_{1}(\textbf{u}_{1}^{t+1})&=\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t+1}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}+\langle\textbf{u}_{1}^{k}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),\textbf{u}_{1}^{t+1}-\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t+1}-\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}\|^{2}\\
&=\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}+\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\langle u_{1i}^{t}-\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\langle\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{k+1}),u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\rangle\end{split}

(18)

Note that
u_{1i}^{t}-\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})=(u_{1i}^{t}-u_{1i}^{t+1})/\gamma_{1}
and
2\langle b-a,a-c\rangle\leq\|b-c\|^{2}-\|a-b\|^{2}-\|a-c\|^{2}
.

\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\langle u_{1i}^{k}-\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\rangle\\
&=\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\langle u_{1i}^{t}-\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{k+1})-u_{1i}^{t}\rangle+\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\langle u_{1i}^{t}-\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),u_{1i}^{t+1}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\rangle\\
&=\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\langle u_{1i}^{t}-\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-u_{1i}^{t}\rangle+\frac{1}{\gamma_{1}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\langle u_{1i}^{t}-u_{1i}^{t+1},u_{1i}^{t+1}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\rangle\\
&\leq\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\langle u_{1i}^{t}-\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-u_{1i}^{t}\rangle\\
&\quad+\frac{1}{2\gamma_{1}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left(\|u_{1i}^{t}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}-\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\|^{2}-\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right)\end{split}

If
\gamma_{1}\leq\frac{1}{5}
, we have

\begin{split}&-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\gamma_{1}}-1-\frac{\gamma_{1}+1}{4\gamma_{1}}\right)\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\|^{2}+\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\langle\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\rangle\\
\leq&-\frac{1}{4\gamma_{1}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\|^{2}+\gamma_{1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left\|\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{4\gamma_{1}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\|^{2}\\
=&\gamma_{1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left\|\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}.\end{split}

Then we can get

\begin{split}\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t+1}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\leq&\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2\gamma_{1}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\\
&-\frac{1}{2\gamma_{1}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\\
&+\gamma_{1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left\|\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}-\frac{\gamma_{1}+1}{8\gamma_{1}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\|^{2}\\
&+\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\langle u_{1i}^{t}-\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-u_{1i}^{t}\rangle.\end{split}

Note that
\frac{1}{2\gamma_{1}}\sum_{i\notin\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}=\frac{1}{2\gamma_{1}}\sum_{i\notin\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}
, which implies that

\frac{1}{2\gamma_{1}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left(\|u_{1i}^{t}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}-\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right)=\frac{1}{2\gamma_{1}}\left(\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}-\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t+1}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right).

Besides, we also have
\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left\|\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\frac{|\mathcal{B}|\sigma_{g}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|}
and

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\langle u_{1i}^{t}-\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-u_{1i}^{t}\rangle\right]&=\frac{|\mathcal{B}|}{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{0}}\langle u_{1i}^{t}-g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),g_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-u_{1i}^{t}\rangle\\
&=-\frac{|\mathcal{B}|}{n_{0}}\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}.\end{split}

Then we can obtain

\begin{split}&\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2\gamma_{1}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t+1}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]\\
\leq&\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2\gamma_{1}}-\frac{|\mathcal{B}|}{n_{0}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]+\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|\sigma_{g}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|}-\frac{\gamma_{1}+1}{8\gamma_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\|^{2}\right].\end{split}

Divide both sides by
\frac{\gamma_{1}+1}{2\gamma_{1}}
we can get

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t+1}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]\leq\frac{\gamma_{1}+1-2\gamma_{1}\frac{|\mathcal{B}|}{n_{0}}}{\gamma_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]&+\frac{2}{\gamma_{1}+1}\frac{\gamma_{1}^{2}|\mathcal{B}|\sigma_{g}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|}\\
&-\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\|^{2}\right].\end{split}

Note that
\frac{\gamma_{1}+1-2\gamma_{1}\frac{|\mathcal{B}|}{n_{0}}}{\gamma_{1}+1}\leq\frac{\gamma_{1}(1-\frac{|\mathcal{B}|}{n_{0}})+1}{\gamma_{1}+1}=1-\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{(\gamma_{1}+1)n_{0}}\leq 1-\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{2n_{0}}
and
\frac{1}{\gamma_{1}+1}\leq 1
for
\gamma_{1}\in(0,1]
. Besides, we have
\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\leq(1+\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{4n_{0}})\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}+(1+\frac{4n_{0}}{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|})\|\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}
due to Young’s inequality,
(1+\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{4n_{0}})(1-\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{2n_{0}})\leq(1-\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{4n_{0}})
and
(1+\frac{4n_{0}}{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|})(1-\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{2n_{0}})\leq\frac{5n_{0}}{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}
.

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\Xi^{t+1}_{1}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n_{0}}\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t+1}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]\\
&\leq\left(1-\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{4n_{0}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n_{0}}\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{t}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}\right]+\frac{5n_{0}L_{g}^{2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}+\frac{2\gamma_{1}^{2}\sigma_{g}^{2}|\mathcal{B}|}{n_{0}|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|}-\frac{1}{4n_{0}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\|^{2}\right]\\
&=\left(1-\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{4n_{0}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\Xi^{t}_{1}\right]+\frac{5n_{0}L_{g}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}]}{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}+\frac{2\gamma_{1}^{2}\sigma_{g}^{2}|\mathcal{B}|}{n_{0}|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|}-\frac{1}{4n_{0}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\|^{2}\right]\end{split}

∎

Lemma 8
.

The gradient variance
\Delta_{2}^{t}:=\|v_{2}^{t}-\nabla H({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}
can be bounded as

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{2}^{t+1}]\leq&(1-\theta)\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{2}^{t}]+\frac{2\beta^{2}L_{H}^{2}}{\theta}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}\right]+5\theta\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\Gamma_{t+1}]\\
&+\frac{3\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\right]+\frac{\theta^{2}\beta^{2}C_{h}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla h}^{2}+L_{h}^{2})}{\min\{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|,|\mathcal{B}_{k}|\}}\end{split}

(19)

}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}+2\left(1+\frac{1}{\theta}\right)\|\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}+\frac{2+3\theta}{\theta}\mathbb{E}_{t}\|\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}+2\mathbb{E}_{t}\|\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}.\end{split}

We can also get

\begin{split}(1+\theta)\|\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}=(1+\theta)(1-\theta)^{2}\|v_{2}^{t}-\nabla H({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}\leq(1-\theta)\|v_{2}^{t}-\nabla H({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}\end{split}

\begin{split}2\left(1+\frac{1}{\theta}\right)\|\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}=&2\left(1+\frac{1}{\theta}\right)(1-\theta)^{2}\|\nabla H({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-\nabla H({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\\
\leq&\frac{2}{\theta}\left\|\frac{1}{m}\sum_{k=1}^{m}\beta\left(\nabla h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})^{\top}[h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}-\nabla h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})^{\top}[h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})]_{+}\right)\right\|^{2}\\
\leq&\frac{2\beta^{2}L_{H}^{2}}{\theta}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}\end{split}

\begin{split}\frac{2+3\theta}{\theta}\|\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}&\leq\frac{2+3\theta}{\theta}\frac{\theta^{2}\beta^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|\nabla\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\|[u_{k}^{t}]_{+}-[h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\|^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{(2+3\theta)\theta\beta^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|\nabla\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\|u_{k}^{t}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\end{split}

Consider that
{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}
and
u_{k}^{t}
do not depend on either
\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}
or
\mathcal{B}_{k}
, we have

\begin{split}&(2+3\theta)\theta\beta^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|\nabla\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\|u_{k}^{t}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]\\
&=(2+3\theta)\theta\beta^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\|\nabla\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}|k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}\right]\|u_{k}^{t}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]\\
&\leq(2+3\theta)\theta\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]\\
&\leq\frac{(2+3\theta)\theta(1+\delta)\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{m}\sum_{k\in[m]}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\|u_{k}^{t+1}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]+\frac{(2+3\theta)\theta(1+1/\delta)\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{m}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\sum_{k\in[m]}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\right]\\
&=\frac{(2+3\theta)\theta(1+\delta)\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{m}\sum_{k\in[m]}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\|u_{k}^{t+1}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]+\frac{(2+3\theta)\theta(1+1/\delta)\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{m}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\right]\end{split}

where the last equation holds by noting that
u_{k}^{t+1}=u_{k}^{t}
for all
i\notin\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}
.

If
\theta\leq\frac{1}{3}
and
\delta=\frac{3\theta}{2}
, we have
(2+3\beta)\beta(1+\delta)\leq 5\theta
and
(2+3\beta)\beta(1+1/\delta)\leq 3
. Therefore, we can get

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2+3\theta}{\theta}\|\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}\right]\leq 5\theta\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\Gamma_{t+1}]+\frac{3\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\right]

Next, we give the upper bound of
\mathbb{E}_{t}\|\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}
.

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{t}\|\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\|^{2}=&\theta^{2}\beta^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}[h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\nabla\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{k=1}^{m}[h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\nabla h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\right]\\
\leq&\theta^{2}\beta^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}[h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\nabla\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}[h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\nabla h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\right]\\
&+\theta^{2}\beta^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}[h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\nabla h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{k=1}^{m}[h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\nabla h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\right]\\
\leq&\frac{\theta^{2}\beta^{2}C_{h}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla h}^{2}+L_{h}^{2})}{\min\{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|,|\mathcal{B}_{k}|\}}\end{split}

Combine above inequalities, we can get

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{2}^{t+1}]\leq&(1-\theta)\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{2}^{t}]+\frac{2\beta^{2}L_{H}^{2}}{\theta}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}\right]+5\theta\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\Gamma_{t+1}]\\
&+\frac{3\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\right]+\frac{\theta^{2}\beta^{2}C_{h}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla h}^{2}+L_{h}^{2})}{\min\{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|,|\mathcal{B}_{k}|\}}.\end{split}

∎

Lemma 9
.

If
\gamma_{2}\leq 1/5
, function value variance
\Gamma_{t}:=\frac{1}{m}\|\textbf{u}^{t}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}
can be bounded as

\mathbb{E}[\Gamma_{t+1}]\leq\left(1-\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{4m}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\Gamma_{t}\right]+\frac{5mL_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}]}{\gamma|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}+\frac{2\gamma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{m|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}-\frac{1}{4m}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\right].

(20)

Proof.

Define
\psi_{k}(\textbf{u})=\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\sum^{m}_{k=1}\|u_{k}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}
, which is
1
-strongly convex.

\begin{split}\psi_{t+1}(\textbf{u}^{t+1})&=\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}^{t+1}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}^{t}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}+\langle\textbf{u}^{t}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),\textbf{u}^{t+1}-\textbf{u}^{t}\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}^{t+1}-\textbf{u}^{t}\|^{2}\\
&=\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}^{t}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}+\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\langle u_{k}^{t}-\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\langle\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\rangle\end{split}

(21)

Note that
u_{k}^{t}-\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})=(q_{i}^{k}-q_{i}^{k+1})/\gamma_{2}
and
2\langle b-a,a-c\rangle\leq\|b-c\|^{2}-\|a-b\|^{2}-\|a-c\|^{2}
.

\begin{split}&\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\langle u_{k}^{t}-\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\rangle\\
&=\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\langle u_{k}^{t}-\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-u_{k}^{t}\rangle+\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\langle u_{k}^{t}-\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),u_{k}^{t+1}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\rangle\\
&=\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\langle u_{k}^{t}-\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-u_{k}^{t}\rangle+\frac{1}{\gamma_{2}}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\langle u_{k}^{t}-u_{k}^{t+1},u_{k}^{t+1}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\rangle\\
&\leq\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\langle u_{k}^{t}-\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-u_{k}^{t}\rangle\\
&\quad+\frac{1}{2\gamma_{2}}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\left(\|u_{k}^{t}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}-\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}-\|u_{k}^{t+1}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right)\end{split}

If
\gamma_{2}\leq\frac{1}{5}
, we have

\begin{split}&-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\gamma_{2}}-1-\frac{\gamma_{2}+1}{4\gamma_{2}}\right)\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}+\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\langle\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\rangle\\
\leq&-\frac{1}{4\gamma_{2}}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}+\gamma_{2}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\left\|\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{4\gamma_{2}}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\\
=&\gamma_{2}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\left\|\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}.\end{split}

Then we can get

\begin{split}\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}^{t+1}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\leq&\frac{1}{2}\|\textbf{u}^{t}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2\gamma_{2}}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\gamma_{2}}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\\
&+\gamma_{2}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\left\|\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}-\frac{\gamma_{2}+1}{8\gamma_{2}}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\\
&+\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{2}^{t+1}}\langle u_{k}^{t}-\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-u_{k}^{t}\rangle.\end{split}

Note that
\frac{1}{2\gamma_{2}}\sum_{k\notin\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}=\frac{1}{2\gamma_{2}}\sum_{k\notin\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}
, which implies that

\frac{1}{2\gamma_{2}}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\left(\|u_{k}^{t}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}-\|u_{k}^{t+1}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right)=\frac{1}{2\gamma_{2}}\left(\|\textbf{u}^{t}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}-\|\textbf{u}^{t+1}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right).

Besides, we also have
\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\left\|\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\frac{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|\sigma_{h}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}
and

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\langle u_{k}^{t}-\hat{h}_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-u_{k}^{t}\rangle\right]&=\frac{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{m}\sum_{k=1}^{m}\langle u_{k}^{t}-h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}),h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-u_{k}^{t}\rangle\\
&=-\frac{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{m}\|\textbf{u}^{t}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}.\end{split}

Then we can obtain

\begin{split}&\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2\gamma_{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\|\textbf{u}^{t+1}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]\\
\leq&\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2\gamma_{2}}-\frac{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{m}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\|\textbf{u}^{t}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]+\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|\sigma_{h}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}-\frac{\gamma_{2}+1}{8\gamma_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\right].\end{split}

Divide both sides by
\frac{\gamma_{2}+1}{2\gamma_{2}}
we can get

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\textbf{u}^{t+1}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]\leq\frac{\gamma_{2}+1-2\gamma_{2}\frac{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{m}}{\gamma_{2}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\textbf{u}^{t}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]&+\frac{2}{\gamma_{2}+1}\frac{\gamma_{2}^{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|\sigma_{h}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}\\
&-\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\right].\end{split}

Note that
\frac{\gamma_{2}+1-2\gamma_{2}\frac{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{m}}{\gamma_{2}+1}\leq\frac{\gamma_{2}(1-\frac{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{m})+1}{\gamma_{2}+1}=1-\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{(\gamma_{2}+1)m}\leq 1-\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{2m}
and
\frac{1}{\gamma_{2}+1}\leq 1
for
\gamma_{2}\in(0,1]
. Besides, we have
\|\textbf{u}^{t}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\leq(1+\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{4m})\|\textbf{u}^{t}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}+(1+\frac{4m}{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|})\|\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}
due to Young’s inequality,
(1+\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{4m})(1-\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{2m})\leq(1-\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{4m})
and
(1+\frac{4m}{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|})(1-\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{2m})\leq\frac{5m}{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}
.

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\Gamma_{t+1}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{m}\|\textbf{u}^{t+1}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\right]\\
&\leq\left(1-\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{4m}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{m}\|\textbf{u}^{t}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}\right]+\frac{5mL_{h}^{2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}+\frac{2\gamma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{m|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}-\frac{1}{4m}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\right]\\
&=\left(1-\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{4m}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\Gamma_{t}\right]+\frac{5mL_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}]}{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}+\frac{2\gamma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{m|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}-\frac{1}{4m}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\|^{2}\right]\end{split}

∎

We state the main theorem again for convenience and present the proof.

Theorem
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
and
4
hold, and set
\beta=\frac{1}{\epsilon\delta}
,

\theta=\min\{\frac{\epsilon^{4}\delta^{2}\min\{|\mathcal{B}_{k}|,|\mathcal{B}_{c}|\}}{672(\sigma_{\nabla h}^{2}+L_{h}^{2})},\frac{\epsilon^{2}\min\{|\mathcal{B}|,|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}{1344L_{f}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla g}^{2}+L_{g}^{2})}\}
,
\gamma_{1}=\gamma_{2}=\min\{\frac{5n_{0}\theta}{3|\mathcal{B}|},\frac{5m\theta}{3|\mathcal{B}_{c}|},\frac{\epsilon^{4}\delta^{2}|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}{26880\sigma_{h}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}\}
and

\eta=\min\left\{\frac{1}{12(L_{F}+\beta L_{H})},\frac{\theta}{8\sqrt{3}L_{F}},\frac{\theta}{8\sqrt{3}L_{H}\beta},\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{40\sqrt{6}L_{g}L_{f}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}n_{0}},\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{40\sqrt{6}\beta L_{h}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}m}\right\}
. Then there exists
\boldsymbol{\lambda}
such that

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})+\nabla\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})\boldsymbol{\lambda})\|\right]\leq\epsilon\\
&\mathbb{E}[\|[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})]_{+}\|]\leq\epsilon\\
&\mathbb{E}[\boldsymbol{\lambda}^{\top}[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})]_{+}]\leq\epsilon\end{split}

with number of iterations
T
of Algorithm
1
bounded by
O(\epsilon^{-7}\delta^{-3})
and
\hat{t}
selected uniformly at random from
\{1,\cdots,T\}
.

Proof.

Since
\Phi({\mathbf{w}})
is
L_{\beta}
-smooth with
L_{\beta}=L_{F}+\beta L_{H}
where
L_{F}:=2(L_{\nabla g}L_{f}+L_{\nabla f}L_{g}^{2})
and
L_{H}:=L_{\nabla h}C_{h}+L_{h}C_{\nabla h}
, we have

\begin{split}\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})&\leq\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})+\langle\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t}),{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\rangle+\frac{L_{\beta}}{2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}\\
&=\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})+\langle v^{t},{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\rangle+\langle\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t},{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\rangle+\frac{L_{\beta}}{2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}\\
&\leq\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})+\langle v^{t},{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\rangle+\left(\frac{L_{\beta}}{2}+\frac{1}{4\eta}\right)\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}+\eta\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}.\end{split}

(22)

Since
{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}={\mathbf{w}}^{t}-\eta v^{t}
, which is equivalent to
{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}=\argmin_{{\mathbf{w}}}\langle v^{t},{\mathbf{w}}\rangle+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|{\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}
, we have

\langle v^{t},{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\rangle\leq-\frac{1}{2\eta}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}.

(23)

Then we can get

\begin{split}\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})&\leq\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})+\left(\frac{L_{\beta}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta}\right)\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}+\eta\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}\end{split}

(24)

\begin{split}\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})&\leq\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})+\left(\frac{L_{\beta}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta}\right)\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}+2\eta\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}-\eta\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}\end{split}

(25)

\begin{split}\eta\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}\leq\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})+\left(\frac{L_{\beta}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta}\right)\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}+2\eta\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}.\end{split}

(26)

Then we want to bound
\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}
.

\begin{split}\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}&=\|(1-\theta)(v_{1}^{t-1}+v_{2}^{t-1})+\theta(G_{1}^{t}+G_{2}^{t})-\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}\\
&=\|(1-\theta)v_{1}^{t-1}+\theta G_{1}^{t}-\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t})+(1-\theta)v_{2}^{t-1}+\theta G_{2}^{t}-\nabla H({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}\\
&=\|v_{1}^{t}-\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t})+v_{2}^{t}-\nabla H({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}\end{split}

(27)

Since
\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[\langle v_{1}^{t}-\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t}),v_{2}^{t}-\nabla H({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\rangle\right]=0
, we have

\mathbb{E}_{t}\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}_{t+1})-v_{t+1}\|^{2}=\mathbb{E}_{t}\|v_{1}^{t+1}-\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}_{t}\|v_{2}^{t+1}-\nabla H({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}

(28)

Summing (
15
),
\frac{20\theta L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}n_{0}}{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}\times
(
16
) and
\frac{20\theta L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}n_{0}}{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}\times
(
17
), we can get

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{1}^{t+1}]\leq&(1-\theta)\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{1}^{t}]+\left(\frac{2L_{F}^{2}}{\theta}+\frac{100\theta L_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}n_{0}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}|\mathcal{B}|^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}[\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}]\\
&+\frac{20\theta L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}n_{0}}{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}\left(1-\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{4n_{0}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\Xi_{1}^{t}+\Xi_{2}^{t}-\Xi^{t+1}_{1}-\Xi^{t+1}_{2}\right]\\
&-L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}\left(\frac{5\theta n_{0}}{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}-3\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\left\|u_{1i}^{t+1}-u_{1i}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left\|u_{2i}^{t+1}-u_{2i}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]\\
&+\frac{2\theta^{2}L_{f}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla g}^{2}+L_{g}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}|,|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}+\frac{80\theta\gamma_{1}\sigma_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}}{\min\{|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}\end{split}

(29)

Summing (
19
) and
\frac{20\theta\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}m}{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\times
(
20
), we can get

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{2}^{t+1}]\leq&(1-\theta)\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{2}^{t}]+\left(\frac{2\beta^{2}L_{H}^{2}}{\theta}+\frac{100\theta\beta^{2}L_{h}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}m^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}[\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}]\\
&+\frac{20\theta\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}m}{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\left(1-\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{4m}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\Gamma_{t}-\Gamma_{t+1}\right]\\
&-\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}\left(\frac{5\theta m}{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}-3\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{m}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{B}_{c}^{t+1}}\left\|u_{k}^{t+1}-u_{k}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]\\
&+\frac{\theta^{2}\beta^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla h}^{2}+L_{h}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|,|\mathcal{B}_{k}|\}}+\frac{40\theta\gamma_{2}\beta^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}\end{split}

(30)

Summing (
26
),
\frac{4\eta}{\theta}\times
(
29
) and
\frac{4\eta}{\theta}\times
(
30
), let
\gamma_{1}=\gamma_{2}=\gamma\leq\min\{\frac{5n_{0}\theta}{3|\mathcal{B}|},\frac{5m\theta}{3|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\}
, we can get
\frac{5\theta n_{0}}{\gamma|\mathcal{B}|}-3\geq 0
,
\frac{5\theta m}{\gamma|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}-3\geq 0
and

\begin{split}&\eta\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v_{t}\|^{2}\\
\leq&\mathbb{E}\left[Y_{t}-Y_{t+1}\right]\\
&-\left(\frac{1}{4\eta}-\frac{L_{F}+\beta L_{H}}{2}-\frac{8\eta L_{F}^{2}}{\theta^{2}}-\frac{400\eta L_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}n_{0}^{2}}{\gamma^{2}|\mathcal{B}|^{2}}-\frac{8\eta\beta^{2}L_{H}^{2}}{\theta^{2}}-\frac{400\eta\beta^{2}L_{h}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}m^{2}}{\gamma^{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}\right]\\
&+\frac{8\eta\theta L_{f}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla g}^{2}+L_{g}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}|,|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}+\frac{320\eta\gamma\sigma_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}}{\min\{|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}+\frac{4\eta\theta\beta^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla h}^{2}+L_{h}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|,|\mathcal{B}_{k}|\}}+\frac{160\eta\gamma\beta^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}\end{split}

(31)

where

Y_{t+1}=\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})+\frac{4\eta}{\theta}\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-v^{t+1}\|^{2}+\frac{80\eta L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}n_{0}}{\gamma|\mathcal{B}|}(\Xi^{t+1}_{1}+\Xi^{t+1}_{2})+\frac{80\eta\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}m}{\gamma|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\Gamma_{t+1}.

If
\eta=\min\left\{\frac{1}{12(L_{F}+\beta L_{H})},\frac{\theta}{8\sqrt{3}L_{F}},\frac{\theta}{8\sqrt{3}L_{H}\beta},\frac{\gamma|\mathcal{B}|}{40\sqrt{6}L_{g}L_{f}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}n_{0}},\frac{\gamma|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{40\sqrt{6}\beta L_{h}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}m}\right\}
, we have

\begin{split}&\frac{\eta}{24}\mathbb{E}\left[\eta^{-2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}+\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v_{t}\|^{2}\right]\\
\leq&\mathbb{E}\left[Y_{t}-Y_{t+1}\right]+\frac{8\eta\theta L_{f}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla g}^{2}+L_{g}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}|,|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}+\frac{320\eta\gamma\sigma_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}}{\min\{|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}+\frac{4\eta\theta\beta^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla h}^{2}+L_{h}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|,|\mathcal{B}_{k}||\}}+\frac{160\eta\gamma\beta^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}.\end{split}

(32)

Dividing both sides by
\frac{\eta}{24}
and taking the average over
T
we can get

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T-1}_{t=0}\mathbb{E}\left[\eta^{-2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}+\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}\right]\\
\leq&\frac{24\mathbb{E}[Y_{0}]}{\eta T}+\frac{192\theta L_{f}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla g}^{2}+L_{g}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}|,|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}+\frac{7680\gamma\sigma_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}}{\min\{|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}+\frac{96\theta\beta^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla h}^{2}+L_{h}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|,|\mathcal{B}_{k}|\}}+\frac{3840\gamma\beta^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}.\end{split}

(33)

\begin{split}Y_{0}&=\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{0})+\frac{4\eta}{\theta}\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{0})-v^{0}\|^{2}+\frac{80\eta L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}n_{0}}{\gamma|\mathcal{B}|}(\Xi_{1}^{0}+\Xi_{2}^{0})+\frac{80\eta\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}m}{\gamma|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\Gamma_{0}\\
&=\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{0})+\frac{4\eta}{\theta}\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{0})-v^{0}\|^{2}+\frac{80\eta L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}}{\gamma|\mathcal{B}|}(\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{0}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{0})\|^{2}+\|\textbf{u}_{2}^{0}-\textbf{g}_{2}({\mathbf{w}}^{0})\|^{2})\\
&\quad\quad\quad+\frac{80\eta\beta^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{\gamma|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}\|\textbf{u}^{0}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{0})\|^{2}.\end{split}

Since
{\mathbf{w}}^{0}
is a feasible solution, we have
\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{0})=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{0}}f(g_{i}({\mathbf{w}}^{0}))
. Since
g
is bounded by Assumption
1
and
f
is Lipschitz continuous, we can show that there exists a constant
C_{F}:=\max\{\tau|\log(c_{g}^{2})|,\tau|\log(C_{g}^{2})|\}
such that
|F(\mathbf{w},\mathcal{D})|\leq C_{F}
. We assume that
u^{0}_{k}=h_{k}({\mathbf{w}}^{0})
,
u^{0}_{1i}=\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{0})
,
u^{0}_{2i}=\hat{g}_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{0})
and
v^{0}=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{0}}(\nabla\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{0})^{\top}\nabla f(\hat{g}_{1i}({\mathbf{w}}^{0})+\nabla\hat{g}_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{0})^{\top}\nabla f(\hat{g}_{2i}({\mathbf{w}}^{0}))
, we can get

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{0})-v^{0}\|^{2}]&\leq 2(C_{\nabla g}^{2}+\sigma_{\nabla g}^{2})C_{\nabla f}^{2}\\
\mathbb{E}[\|\textbf{u}_{1}^{0}-\textbf{g}_{1}({\mathbf{w}}^{0})\|^{2}]&\leq\sigma_{g}^{2}\\
\mathbb{E}[\|\textbf{u}_{2}^{0}-\textbf{g}_{2}({\mathbf{w}}^{0})\|^{2}]&\leq\sigma_{g}^{2}\\
\mathbb{E}[\|\textbf{u}^{0}-\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{0})\|^{2}]&=0\end{split}

(34)

Therefore, we can get

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T-1}_{t=0}\mathbb{E}\left[\eta^{-2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}+\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}\right]\\
\leq&\frac{24C_{F}}{\eta T}+\frac{192(C_{\nabla g}^{2}+\sigma_{\nabla g}^{2})C_{\nabla f}^{2}}{\theta T}+\frac{1920L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}\sigma_{g}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}|\gamma T}\\
&+\frac{192\theta L_{f}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla g}^{2}+L_{g}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}|,|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}+\frac{7680\gamma\sigma_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}^{2}}{\min\{|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}+\frac{96\theta\beta^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla h}^{2}+L_{h}^{2})}{\text{min}\{|\mathcal{B}_{c}|,|\mathcal{B}_{k}|\}}+\frac{3840\gamma\beta^{2}\sigma_{h}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}{|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}.\end{split}

(35)

Let
\beta=\frac{1}{\epsilon\delta}
,
\theta=\min\left\{\frac{\epsilon^{4}\delta^{2}\min\{|\mathcal{B}_{k}|,|\mathcal{B}_{c}|\}}{672(\sigma_{\nabla h}^{2}+L_{h}^{2})},\frac{\epsilon^{2}\min\{|\mathcal{B}|,|\mathcal{B}_{1i}|,|\mathcal{B}_{2i}|\}}{1344L_{f}^{2}(\sigma_{\nabla g}^{2}+L_{g}^{2})}\right\}=O(\epsilon^{4}\delta^{2})
,

\gamma_{1}=\gamma_{2}=\gamma\leq\min\left\{\frac{5n_{0}\theta}{3|\mathcal{B}|},\frac{5m\theta}{3|\mathcal{B}_{c}|},\frac{\epsilon^{4}\delta^{2}|\mathcal{B}_{k}|}{26880\sigma_{h}^{2}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}^{2}}\right\}=O(\epsilon^{4}\delta^{2})
,

\eta=\min\left\{\frac{1}{12(L_{F}+\beta L_{H})},\frac{\theta}{8\sqrt{3}L_{F}},\frac{\theta}{8\sqrt{3}L_{H}\beta},\frac{\gamma_{1}|\mathcal{B}|}{40\sqrt{6}L_{g}L_{f}\tilde{C}_{\nabla g}n},\frac{\gamma_{2}|\mathcal{B}_{c}|}{40\sqrt{6}\beta L_{h}\tilde{C}_{\nabla h}m}\right\}=O(\epsilon^{5}\delta^{3})
and

T=O(\epsilon^{-7}\delta^{-3})
, we have

\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T-1}_{t=0}\mathbb{E}\left[\eta^{-2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}+\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}\right]\\
\leq O(\epsilon^{2})

By the definition of
{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}
, we have

\begin{split}&{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}+\eta v^{t}=0\\
&\Leftrightarrow\eta^{-1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})+(\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t})+(\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t}))=\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\\
&\Leftrightarrow\eta^{-1}({\mathbf{w}}^{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})+(\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t})+(\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t}))\\
&\quad\quad\quad=\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})+\frac{\beta}{m}\nabla\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\end{split}

This gives

\begin{split}&\quad\left\|\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})+\frac{\beta}{m}\nabla\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})^{\top}[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\right\|^{2}\\
&\leq 3\left(\eta^{-2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}\|^{2}+\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}+\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})\|^{2}\right)\\
&\leq 3\left(\eta^{-2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}\|^{2}+\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}+L_{\beta}^{2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}\|^{2}\right)\\
&\leq 3\left(\eta^{-2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}\|^{2}+\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2}+\eta^{-2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}\|^{2}\right)\\
&\leq 6(\eta^{-2}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}\|^{2}+\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{w}}^{t})-v^{t}\|^{2})\end{split}

Therefore, we can achieve that

\begin{split}\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T-1}_{t=0}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})+\frac{\beta}{m}\nabla\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq O(\epsilon^{2})\end{split}

(36)

By Jensen’s inequality, we can get

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})+\frac{\beta}{m}\nabla\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})]_{+}\right\|\right]\leq O(\epsilon),\end{split}

(37)

with
\hat{t}
selected uniformly at random from
\{1,\cdots,T\}
.

Then, with the full rank assumption on the Jacobian, which is
\|\nabla\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})]_{+}\|\geq\delta\|[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t})]_{+}\|
as in Assumption
4
, we can get

\begin{split}\|[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\|^{2}\leq&\frac{1}{\delta^{2}}\|\nabla\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\|^{2}\\
=&\frac{m^{2}}{\beta^{2}\delta^{2}}\|\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})+\frac{\beta}{m}\nabla\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}-\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\|^{2}\\
\leq&\frac{2m^{2}}{\beta^{2}\delta^{2}}\left[\left\|\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})+\frac{\beta}{m}\nabla\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\right\|^{2}\right]\end{split}

(38)

Taking the average over
T
, we can get

\begin{split}\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T-1}_{t=0}\mathbb{E}\|[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\|^{2}\leq&\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T-1}_{t=0}\frac{2m^{2}}{\beta^{2}\delta^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla F({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})+\frac{\beta}{m}\nabla\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})]_{+}\right\|^{2}\right]\\
\leq&O(\epsilon^{2})\end{split}

(39)

and using
\boldsymbol{\lambda}=\frac{\beta}{m}[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})]_{+}
. By Jensen’s inequality, we can get

\mathbb{E}\|[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})]_{+}\|\leq O(\epsilon)

(40)

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}|\boldsymbol{\lambda}^{\top}[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})]_{+}|=\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{\beta}{m}[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})]_{+}^{\top}[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})]_{+}\right|&=\frac{\beta}{m}\mathbb{E}\|[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})]_{+}\|^{2}\\
&=\frac{1}{m\delta\epsilon}\mathbb{E}\|[\textbf{h}({\mathbf{w}}^{\hat{t}})]_{+}\|^{2}\\
&\leq O(\epsilon).\end{split}

(41)

∎
